**Layout**

Three horizontal bands: a light grey header band (top 0 to 20.5%) holding the title and subtitle, a white middle band (20.5% to 86.1%) holding the chart, and a light grey footer band (86.1% to 100%) holding the source note, author, date and page number. Title and subtitle are left-aligned on a shared left edge at x 20.5%. The chart sits left-aligned at x 20.5% and spans to about x 95.4%, with the chart title above it at y 27.4%. Source lines sit at the left margin x 20.5% in the footer; author, date and page number sit to the right, with the page number at x 94.3%.

**Visuals**

Single horizontal 100% stacked bar chart (one bar) titled 'Global ad revenue, 2024 ($bn)'. Horizontal value axis labelled only by tick values from 0 to 1,100 in steps of 100, with no axis title and no unit label beyond the chart title ($bn); vertical dotted light-grey gridlines at each 100 step, and a solid mid-grey vertical axis line at 0. The bar spans y 49.6% to 67.0% of the slide height. Segments left to right, with about values estimated because no values are printed: Alphabet about 264 (red #EB483F, label 'Alphabet' in white), Meta about 161 (coral #F0766F, label 'Meta' in white), Amazon about 57 (pale pink #F8DBD8, label 'Amazon' rotated 90 degrees reading upward, in #535353), All other ex-China about 376 (dark grey #535353, label 'All other ex-China' in white), China about 178 (white fill with thin dark grey #535353 outline, label 'China' in #535353). The segments end at about 1,036 in total. Thin white separators of about 0.1% width sit between the coloured segments. No legend, no highlighted points beyond the colour coding, no logos or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid #F1F1F1 light grey covering the full slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from 20.5% to 86.1% of the slide height. Flat, minimal consulting-style look. Header and footer bands #F1F1F1 with a white #FFFFFF chart band between them. Title in a serif display face (MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional serif similar to Georgia-like book serifs), regular weight, colour #545454. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454. Chart title and all chart and footer text in a geometric grotesque sans (MessinaSans family): chart title SemiBold #545554, segment labels Regular 1.85% of slide height, tick labels Regular #545454 or #545554, author name Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number Book #A6A6A6. Title text size about 5.6% of slide height; subtitle about 2.6%; body and labels about 1.9%. Accent colours: red #EB483F, coral #F0766F, pale pink #F8DBD8, dark grey #535353. Shapes are square-cornered rectangles with no shadows or gradients; gridlines are dotted and light grey.

### Slide 50

**Text**

And what kinds of questions are these?
Tech changes the landscape for each industry, and then moves on
How does AI
search &
discovery
change retail?
What will AI-
enabled robotics
do to logistics?
How will AI
video generation
change
Hollywood?
Benedict Evans
–– May 2025
50

**Layout**

Three equal circles sit in one horizontal row across the middle-to-lower slide, evenly spaced and centred in the content area. The title is left-aligned at about 20.5% from the left, with the subtitle directly beneath it. The footer line (author, date, page number) sits bottom right at about 91% from the top, right-aligned to roughly 98.8% of the width.

**Visuals**

Three solid red filled circles of identical size, each about 22.1% wide and 39.3% high, with no outline, shadow or gradient. Circle 1 (left) reads: How does AI / search & / discovery / change retail? Circle 2 (centre) reads: What will AI- / enabled robotics / do to logistics? Circle 3 (right) reads: How will AI / video generation / change / Hollywood? All three are white serif text centred inside the circles. No photographs, charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide. Flat minimal look on a light grey ground. Title and subtitle are set in a serif (MessinaModern-Regular) in dark grey #545454. Circle text is a serif book weight (MessinaModern-Book) in white #FFFFFF. Footer is a sans-serif (MessinaSans) in light grey #A6A6A6, with the author name in bold and the date and page number in book weight. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle text about 3.3%, footer about 1.9%. Accent colour is red #EB483F. Shapes are perfect circles with no corner radius, shadow or gradient.

### Slide 51

**Text**

Welcome to the next / platform shift?
Benedict Evans –– May 2025 51

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slate background with a single lighter translucent panel occupying the right and central area, spanning from about 17% to 96% horizontally and 20% to 81% vertically. The title sits top-left inside the panel, left-aligned at about 20% from the left edge, over two lines. A thin white horizontal rule runs along the bottom edge of the panel. The footer, in small grey type, is right-aligned at the bottom right with the author name, a dash and date, and the slide number at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background is a flat dark slate-teal (#36494E). A translucent white panel (about 4% opacity, rendering as #3E5058) sits over it with square corners and no shadow. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional serif, at about 9.3% of slide height, regular weight, sentence case, not capitalised. A white horizontal rule about 0.3% of slide height thick runs along the panel's bottom edge. No gradients, no corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 52

**Text**

Posit: all AI questions have one of two answers
Will this just be like everything else? No-one really knows
Answer:
“It will work like every other platform shift”
Insert your question here…
Answer:
“No-one knows”
Benedict Evans
–– May 2025
52

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 20.5% of slide width, with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a shared left edge. Three equal circles (each about 22% wide) sit in one horizontal row centred vertically on the slide, with about 5% gaps; small grey triangles fill the gaps between circles. The footer line sits bottom-right at about 91% of slide height, with the slide number at the far right edge (about 94% to 95.5% x).

**Visuals**

Three filled circles in a horizontal row, all the same size and vertically aligned. Left circle: dark grey #535353, white serif text 'Answer:' above the quotation 'It will work like every other platform shift'. Centre circle: red #EB483F, white serif text 'Insert your question here…' (a placeholder). Right circle: dark grey #535353, white serif text 'Answer:' above the quotation 'No-one knows'. Between the left and centre circles is a small left-pointing solid grey triangle (#A6A6A6); between the centre and right circles is a small right-pointing solid grey triangle (#A6A6A6), both with flat edges facing the circles. No photographs, illustrations, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat design with no gradients, shadows or outlines on any shape. Background light grey #F1F1F1. Title in a serif face (MessinaModern-Regular, a Modern-style serif, regular weight) at about 5.56% of slide height, colour #545454. Subtitle in the same serif at about 2.59% of slide height, colour #545454. Circle labels in MessinaModern-Book serif at about 3.33% of slide height, white #FFFFFF. Footer in MessinaSans sans-serif at about 1.85% of slide height: 'Benedict Evans' in Bold, the date and slide number in Book, all colour #A6A6A6. Circles are perfect ellipses with no corner radius; triangles are sharp-cornered isosceles shapes.

### Slide 53

**Text**

But no-one ever knows / What did we know about the future of the internet? / Source: TeleGeography / Figure 5. Network Gateways: A Cyberspace Divided / In this map, the major computer networks huddle in the mass of the Matrix, the term for the global collection of computer networks that can exchange electronic mail. The Internet serves as a common ground for much communication on-line, with commercial on-line services building gateways for electronic mail as well as other communication and data protocols to the Internet. Major national services such as France's Minitel (http://www.minitel.fr/) now provide gateway communication from their services to the Internet. / © TeleGeography, Inc. 1995 / Other Nets / private / secure / The Matrix / Usenet / UUCP / Minitel / Private Nets / Internet / Gopher space / Telnet space / Web space / FTP space / WAIS space / FidoNet / BITNET / America Online / Delphi / Prodigy / GEnie / Commercial Services / Compuserve / Network Gateway / Benedict Evans / –– May 2025 / 53

**Layout**

Two-column split at 50% of slide width: a white left panel holds the title, subtitle and source line, and a light grey right panel holds one framed figure. The title sits in the left column at about 25.6% from top, left-aligned at a left margin of about 4.6%, with the subtitle below it. The source line sits bottom-left at about 91% height. The footer runs along the bottom of the right panel: author name, dash and date left of the slide number, which sits at about 94% width.

**Visuals**

One scanned printed figure (a diagram, not a photograph) placed in the right panel, shown as a white page with a soft drop shadow and small solid black corner marks at the top-left and bottom-right of the scan. The page holds a bold caption title 'Figure 5. Network Gateways: A Cyberspace Divided', a paragraph of small black body text, and a black-outline cloud-style network map. Shapes and labels: a large irregular blob labelled 'The Matrix' encloses several smaller shapes. Inside it, 'Usenet' is a diamond, 'UUCP' and 'Minitel' are small rounded blobs at upper right, 'Private Nets' and 'FidoNet' are blobs at left, and 'Internet' is a large blob with 'Gopher space', 'Telnet space', 'Web space', 'FTP space' and 'WAIS space' written inside it. 'BITNET' is a blob at lower left. 'Commercial Services' is a blob at lower right containing 'America Online', 'Delphi', 'Prodigy', 'GEnie' and 'Compuserve'. A separate blob at the top, labelled 'Other Nets', contains 'private' and 'secure'. Connections are dotted double-headed arrows, each with a hollow open-triangle marker, linking the blobs. A legend at lower left shows a double-headed dotted arrow with a triangle marker labelled 'Network Gateway'. Everything is black line art on white paper.

**Design**

Background: Left half solid white #FFFFFF, right half solid light grey #F2F2F2. Two flat fills: the right panel and full background are light grey #F2F2F2 and the left panel is white #FFFFFF, with no gradient. Title and subtitle text are dark grey #545454 in a regular-weight Messina Modern serif. The source line is dark grey #545454 in a sans-serif book weight (Messina Sans). Footer text is light grey #A6A6A6 in sans-serif, with the name in bold. Title text is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 3.0%, and source and footer about 1.85%. The framed figure has a soft drop shadow, square corners, and black corner marks on the scan.

54

**Layout**

Full-bleed light grey (#F1F1F1) header band across the top of the slide, with a white body panel from about 20.5% to 86.1% of slide height and a light grey footer band below it. The title is left-aligned at about 20.5% x and 5.6% y, with the subtitle beneath it at the same left edge. The line chart sits on the white panel with its chart title at the left edge of the plot, a legend in the left margin at about 4.8% x, and x-axis labels beneath the plot. The source and footnote sit bottom-left at about 20.5% x in the footer band, and the author, date and slide number sit bottom-right with the page number at about 94.3% x.

**Visuals**

One line chart titled 'E-commerce as % US retail sales'. Y axis: percentage from 0% to 25% in 5% steps, labelled 0%, 5%, 10%, 15%, 20%, 25%, with no unit beyond the percent sign. X axis: time from Mar 2000 to Mar 2025, labelled every five years (Mar 2000, Mar 2005, Mar 2010, Mar 2015, Mar 2020, Mar 2025). Gridlines are dotted light grey horizontal lines at each 5% step, and the baseline at 0% is a solid thin grey line. Legend at upper left of the plot: dark grey line marked 'Addressable retail*' and red line marked 'Retail'. Series 'Addressable retail*' (dark grey #545454): about 1% in Mar 2000, about 3% in Mar 2005, about 5.5% at the 2008 peak then a dip to about 5% around Mar 2009, about 6% in Mar 2010, about 10% in Mar 2016, about 17% in Mar 2020, a jump to about 21% in early 2020, about 21% to 22% through 2021 to 2023, and about 23% in Mar 2025. Series 'Retail' (red #E8453C): about 0.6% in Mar 2000, about 3% in Mar 2005, about 4% around Mar 2009, about 7% in Mar 2014, about 10% in Mar 2019, a jump to about 14.5% in 2020, about 14% to 15% through 2021 to 2023, and about 16% in Mar 2025. Neither series has printed data labels, so all values are estimates. No points are highlighted.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide background with a white #FFFFFF panel across the middle. Light grey (#F1F1F1) top and bottom bands with a white middle panel (#FFFFFF). Title in MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional serif, regular weight, dark grey #545454. Subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular at small size, #545454. Chart title and legend and axis labels in MessinaSans, a sans-serif, with the chart title in SemiBold and the rest Regular, dark grey #545454. Source and footnote in MessinaSans Regular #545454. Author name in MessinaSans Bold #A6A6A6 with date and page number in MessinaSans Book #A6A6A6. Title size is about 5.6% of slide height, body and labels about 1.9%. Series lines are about 0.3% of slide height thick, round-capped, with no fill, no shadows and no gradients. Gridlines are dotted #A6A6A6 hairlines. Accent colour is red #E8453C for the Retail series.

55

**Layout**

Full-width header band with the serif title at left-aligned x 20.5% and the subtitle directly below it, both starting at the same left edge. A white content panel spans the middle of the slide from y 20.5% to 86.1%, holding a chart chart title at x 20.6% and a legend at the far left (x 4.8%). The chart plot runs from x about 15.7% to 95.3%, with the x-axis labels beneath it. The footer band at the bottom holds the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and slide number at right, with the page number 55 at x 94.3%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Monthly ‘robotaxi’ trips in California'. Y axis: monthly trips, unlabelled units, range 0 to 800,000 in gridlines every 100,000, dotted light grey horizontal gridlines. X axis: months, labelled at Sep 2023, Dec 2023, Mar 2024, Jun 2024, Sep 2024, Dec 2024, Mar 2025, with one point per month from about Aug 2023 to Mar 2025. Series Waymo (red #EE4035 line, red filled circle markers about 2.5% of slide width): about 12,000 (Aug 2023), about 38,000, about 57,000, about 57,000, about 73,000, about 78,000, about 75,000, about 84,000, about 92,000, about 145,000, about 190,000, about 252,000, about 312,000, about 355,000, about 453,000, about 503,000, about 542,000, about 550,000, about 560,000, about 708,000 (Mar 2025, the highest point, marked at the top right end). Series Cruise (dark grey #4D4D4D line and markers): about 10,000, about 12,000, about 11,000, about 1,000, ending at about Nov 2023, with no points after that. Tesla has a legend entry (light grey #B0B0B0 marker) but no plotted line or points. Legend at upper left of chart, vertical stack of three entries with circle-and-line markers: Waymo, Cruise, Tesla. No highlighted points beyond the markers; the axis baseline is a thin grey line at 0.

**Design**

### Slide 56

**Text**

From dreams to deployment
What’s already big, what’s being built, and what comes next?
Dreams from
2005
E-commerce,
streaming, SaaS,
workflows
2025
Generative AI!
Dreams for
2030
XR, crypto,
robotics, space,
quantum, nuclear,
comp-bio, AVs
Benedict Evans
–– May 2025
56

**Layout**

Three equal white circles sit in a horizontal row across the middle of the slide, evenly spaced, with grey left-pointing triangles between them, and the title and subtitle are left-aligned at the top starting at about 20.5% of the slide width. The footer runs along the bottom right with the author name, date and slide number on one baseline, and a wide empty margin lies to the left of the diagram.

**Visuals**

Diagram: three white circles with thin dark grey outlines (about 0.3% of slide width) in a left-to-right row, with the left and middle circles outlined #545454 and the right circle outlined red #E8473F. Two solid grey left-pointing isosceles triangles sit in the gaps between circles: one between the 2005 circle and the 2025 circle, one between the 2025 circle and the 2030 circle, each pointing left toward the earlier circle. Circle 1 reads 'Dreams from 2005' with 'E-commerce, streaming, SaaS, workflows' beneath it. Circle 2 reads '2025' with 'Generative AI!' beneath it. Circle 3 reads 'Dreams for 2030' with 'XR, crypto, robotics, space, quantum, nuclear, comp-bio, AVs' beneath it. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat light grey background with no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular serif (Messina Modern, a Scotch-style book serif), colour #545454 dark grey. Circle headings in MessinaModern-SemiBold, body in MessinaModern-Book/Regular, all #545454. Title about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle headings about 3.3%, circle body about 3.0%, footer about 1.9%. Footer in MessinaSans-Bold for the name and MessinaSans-Book for the date and number, colour #A6A6A6. Circle outlines are thin 2-3 px strokes with no fill shadow; the red outline highlights the 2030 circle. Triangles are flat #A6A6A6 with no outline. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.
